- **aider**: This setup writes local instructions for Aider; it does not provide a Room MCP client or start an agent. - **aider**: Aider has no plugin system — only a notification command that fires, without payload, whenever the LLM finishes and waits for input. So this adapter is a launch wrapper with honest, limited fidelity: presence in the room, and a nudge when Aider wants you. ([cfoust/antiphon](https://github.com/cfoust/antiphon/blob/HEAD/docs/docs/agents/aider.md) · ★1)
- **aider**: Aider 沒有外掛系統——只有一個通知指令，會在 LLM 完成並等待輸入時觸發，且不帶任何內容。因此這個轉接器是一個啟動包裝器，保真度有限但誠實：在房間裡的存在感，以及 Aider 需要你時的一聲輕推。 ([cfoust/antiphon](https://github.com/cfoust/antiphon/blob/HEAD/docs/i18n/zh-Hant/docusaurus-plugin-content-docs/current/agents/aider.md) · ★1)
- **aider**: Aider 没有插件系统——只有一条通知命令，在 LLM 结束并等待输入时触发，且不携带任何负载。因此这个适配器是一个启动包装器，保真度有限但诚实：在房间里有一席之地，外加 Aider 需要你时的一声轻推。 ([cfoust/antiphon](https://github.com/cfoust/antiphon/blob/HEAD/docs/i18n/zh-Hans/docusaurus-plugin-content-docs/current/agents/aider.md) · ★1)
- **aider**: У Aider нет системы плагинов — только команда уведомления, которая срабатывает, без полезной нагрузки, всякий раз, когда LLM закончил и ждёт ввода. Поэтому этот адаптер — обёртка запуска с честной, но ограниченной полнотой: присутствие в комнате и мягкий толчок, когда Aider вас ждёт. ([cfoust/antiphon](https://github.com/cfoust/antiphon/blob/HEAD/docs/i18n/ru/docusaurus-plugin-content-docs/current/agents/aider.md) · ★1)

- **aind-reviewer**: Cold, independent reviewer of a code PR. Posts resolvable review threads for blocking findings, a summary comment, and returns a structured verdict. Never authors fixes. - **aist-adjudicator**: Judges an author's written reason for rejecting or escalating a review finding, and returns upheld, overturned or escalate. Use for every rejected or escalated finding under `.sdlc/review-process.md`; it does not review the change itself. ([aist-ai-sast/aist](https://github.com/aist-ai-sast/aist/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/aist-adjudicator.md))
- **aist-api-reviewer**: Reviews a change to AIST endpoints, authorization or the data model for organization isolation — the right ResourcePolicy and named Action, scoped lookups including nested ones, token scope, and superuser behaviour in the registered getter — beyond what the committed authz lint gates already decide. Use when `python3 .sdlc/route.py` lists `api` for a change, before that change is ready to commit. ([aist-ai-sast/aist](https://github.com/aist-ai-sast/aist/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/aist-api-reviewer.md))
- **aist-architecture-docs-maintainer**: Maintains AIST reader-facing architecture, data-flow, and threat-model documentation from verified code changes. In audit mode it is the `docs` reviewer `.sdlc/scope.yaml` routes changed pages to, falsifying each claim against the implementation. ([aist-ai-sast/aist](https://github.com/aist-ai-sast/aist/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/aist-architecture-docs-maintainer.md))
- **aist-architecture-reviewer**: Judges the boundaries a change touches — analyzer logic outside sast-pipeline, computation in client-ui, edits to or private use of vendor/, access decided outside aist/authz, writers bypassing a store's owner, public signatures broken for other components — by connascence. Use when `python3 .sdlc/route.py` lists this reviewer for a change, before that change is ready to commit. ([aist-ai-sast/aist](https://github.com/aist-ai-sast/aist/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/aist-architecture-reviewer.md))
- **aist-code-reviewer**: Reviews one change against the coding rules it owns in `.sdlc/coding-rules.md` — code that does not do what it says, non-idempotent tasks, unstructured cross-component values, pattern-matched formats, hardcoded policy, swallowed failures, broken callers, missing outcome tests — and routes sensitive seams to the security reviewers. Use when `python3 .sdlc/route.py` lists this reviewer for a change, before that change is ready to commit. ([aist-ai-sast/aist](https://github.com/aist-ai-sast/aist/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/aist-code-reviewer.md))
- **aist-comment-reviewer**: Reads every comment and docstring in the changed units against the code it sits on, and flags comments that narrate history instead of stating the contract, state something the code does not do, or restate the code. Use when `python3 .sdlc/route.py` lists this reviewer for a change, before that change is ready to commit. ([aist-ai-sast/aist](https://github.com/aist-ai-sast/aist/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/aist-comment-reviewer.md))
- **aist-migration-validator**: Validates Django model and migration changes in aist/ for safety. Checks for data loss risk, backward compatibility, deduplication hash field consistency, and MCP pipeline resolution integrity. Use when `python3 .sdlc/route.py` lists `migration` for a change, before that change is ready to commit. ([aist-ai-sast/aist](https://github.com/aist-ai-sast/aist/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/aist-migration-validator.md))
- **aist-regression-guard**: Traces a change to the user-visible outcomes it can affect and judges whether a test would fail if each one broke, including capabilities silently lost while generalising a feature and tenant-isolation outcomes with no second-organization test. Use when `python3 .sdlc/route.py` lists this reviewer for a change, before that change is ready to commit. ([aist-ai-sast/aist](https://github.com/aist-ai-sast/aist/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/aist-regression-guard.md))
- **aist-reuse-reviewer**: Reads its slice of the reuse read set in full, inventories what every definition does, and rules each definition in the change a duplicate, an extension or new — catching semantic duplicates under another name, a second mechanism beside an existing one, and hand-written code where a maintained library or a `vendor/` definition already does the job. Use when `python3 .sdlc/route.py` lists this reviewer for a change, before that change is ready to commit. ([aist-ai-sast/aist](https://github.com/aist-ai-sast/aist/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/aist-reuse-reviewer.md))
- **aist-security-checker**: Fast security reviewer for new features and high-risk changes. Use when a diff changes auth, tenant scoping, queries, secrets, file access, subprocesses, outbound networking, runtime/container behavior, or async result flows. Use when `python3 .sdlc/route.py` lists `security` for a change, before that change is ready to commit. ([aist-ai-sast/aist](https://github.com/aist-ai-sast/aist/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/aist-security-checker.md))
- **aist-structure-reviewer**: Judges what a change did to structure: modules with two reasons to change, rules smeared across view, serializer and query, functions with modes, raw dicts that should be dataclasses, and bare strings that should be enums. Use when `python3 .sdlc/route.py` lists this reviewer for a change, before that change is ready to commit. ([aist-ai-sast/aist](https://github.com/aist-ai-sast/aist/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/aist-structure-reviewer.md))
- **aist-test-value-reviewer**: Judges whether each test a change adds or alters is evidence of behaviour — a real Act through a public interface, an independent expected value, a user scenario — and flags tests of file text, of the test machinery, of recorded run data, or carrying production values. Use when `python3 .sdlc/route.py` lists this reviewer for a change, before that change is ready to commit. ([aist-ai-sast/aist](https://github.com/aist-ai-sast/aist/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/aist-test-value-reviewer.md))
- **aist-ui-security-checker**: Fast security reviewer for client-ui React changes that alter permission-gated UI, mutating controls, or rendering of server-supplied data. Use when `python3 .sdlc/route.py` lists `ui-security` for a change, before that change is ready to commit. ([aist-ai-sast/aist](https://github.com/aist-ai-sast/aist/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/aist-ui-security-checker.md))
- **aistart360-ai-pipeline-engineer**: AIStart360 document-intelligence specialist. Use when work touches `lib/documents/` (parse.ts, extract.ts, chunking.ts, future bind-fields.ts / embed.ts), `lib/ai/openrouter.ts`, OpenRouter prompts, Claude Sonnet 4.5 calls, embeddings, semantic chunking, RAG, or the `parsed_data` JSON shape. Knows that parsing goes via OpenRouter (`anthropic/claude-sonnet-4.5`), not the native Anthropic SDK, and that `DocumentChunk.embedding vector(1536)` is currently unpopulated. ([TechNomadJourneyMan/AIStart360.app_ClientSuperSystemHyperApp](https://github.com/TechNomadJourneyMan/AIStart360.app_ClientSuperSystemHyperApp/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/aistart360-ai-pipeline-engineer.md) · ★2)
- **aistart360-data-engineer**: AIStart360 data-layer specialist. Use when the task touches Prisma schema, Supabase tables (profiles/companies/survey_answers/documents/metrics/diagnostics), RLS policies, the metric resolver / materialize layer (`lib/metrics/`), or any migration in `supabase/migrations/`. Knows that Prisma and Supabase share one Postgres but own different tables, and that `public.metrics` is the materialization target. Apply migrations to a local/test database with `node scripts/apply-migration.js &lt;file&gt; --yes`; production goes only through the bundle (`scripts/deploy/migrations-bundle.mjs`, docs/deploy/runb… ([TechNomadJourneyMan/AIStart360.app_ClientSuperSystemHyperApp](https://github.com/TechNomadJourneyMan/AIStart360.app_ClientSuperSystemHyperApp/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/aistart360-data-engineer.md) · ★2)
- **aistart360-realtime-engineer**: AIStart360 realtime / analytics-engine specialist. Use for Supabase Realtime channel wiring (`supabase.channel().on('postgres_changes', ...)`), the `useRealtimeSync` hook, anomaly detection (rolling z-score), forecast engines (linear regression + EMA), and the API routes `/api/v1/metrics/[id]/{timeseries,forecast,anomalies}`. Knows that migration 016 enabled Realtime on `metrics`, `diagnostics`, `documents` with REPLICA IDENTITY FULL. ([TechNomadJourneyMan/AIStart360.app_ClientSuperSystemHyperApp](https://github.com/TechNomadJourneyMan/AIStart360.app_ClientSuperSystemHyperApp/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/aistart360-realtime-engineer.md) · ★2)
- **aistart360-ui-engineer**: AIStart360 UI specialist. Use for any component in `components/`, any page under `app/(dashboard)/`, `app/client/`, design tokens, or anything that must match the existing premium dark glassmorphism + teal `#6effc0` brand. Knows the Material Design 3 token system, Framer Motion patterns, the existing `PointARadarWidget`, and that all UI copy is Russian. ([TechNomadJourneyMan/AIStart360.app_ClientSuperSystemHyperApp](https://github.com/TechNomadJourneyMan/AIStart360.app_ClientSuperSystemHyperApp/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/aistart360-ui-engineer.md) · ★2)

- **aitp-reader**: Independent reader without the conversation, for AITP research. Three modes - claim (check a stated claim by its own route, without seeing its derivation), story (answer a topic's questions from its story.md alone) and skill-trial (apply a Skill to a new case). Use before a claim is presented as established, when sources disagree, at a story milestone, or to test a distilled Skill. Returns verdicts with locators; writes nothing into the research tree. - **ajtbd-canon-validator**: Pre-publication QA gate for Discovery reports. Validates a report against the Advanced Jobs To Be Done canon by Ivan Zamesin (NOT generic Christensen JTBD) — checks that each Job is named correctly (`I want to + infinitive verb`, one verb, a verb phrase and not a noun), that Full Jobs carry all 8 elements, that Big/Core/Small/Micro levels are distinguished correctly (Small = sibling of Core, not below it), and that success criteria are concrete, measurable and justified. Returns a PASS / BLOCK verdict with a list of findings. Does NOT rewrite the report. Use before publishing any Discovery re… ([VladislavDerkach/product-discovery-flow](https://github.com/VladislavDerkach/product-discovery-flow/blob/HEAD/files/agents/ajtbd-canon-validator.md) · ★1)
- **ajtbd-segmentation-agent**: Segments customers using the Advanced Jobs To Be Done (AJTBD) methodology by Ivan Zamesin — NOT generic Christensen JTBD. Builds the Job Graph (Big Job → Core Job → Small Job → Micro Job, where Small Jobs are siblings of Core Jobs, not subordinates), clusters people into segments by Job Graph similarity (similar Core Jobs + similar success criteria), and assesses each segment on value creation, profitability, scalability, and the Critical Chain. Use when you need to identify who to build for, what MVP to launch, or how to prioritize growth strategy. ([VladislavDerkach/product-discovery-flow](https://github.com/VladislavDerkach/product-discovery-flow/blob/HEAD/files/agents/ajtbd-segmentation-agent.md) · ★1)
